## Levels and naming

- **Directory split (2026-09-30):** the maintainer requested `davinci/` for levels, guest, dialect and extension infrastructure; Carton and legacy products stay in `crates/`, in one PR. The [directory record](./2026-09-30-davinci-directory.md) defines the boundary and replay commands. This program root is the explicit exception to the codename directory rule.
- The stages are renamed **L0–L4** (levels). The rename is in progress.
- **Internal Davinci crates are named by level only**: `vize_l0` … `vize_l4`, and conversions `vize_l1_to_l2` and `vize_l2_to_l3`. `vize_l0_derive` stays separate for proc macros. The independent external Rust guest product is the explicit `vize_guest` exception ([decision](./2026-09-28-guest-sdk-crate-axis.md)).
- There are no functional internal crate names (no `vize_folio`, `vize_dialect`, …). Internal functional concerns are modules inside a level crate.
- **Codenames are aliases only.** Davinci, Sinopia, Disegno, Ricalco, Impeto, Folio, Spolvero and the rest may appear in crate docs (`//! L3 — reactivity IR (codename: Impeto)`) and in `docs/`. They never appear in crate, directory, file, module or type names, or in serialized strings. Ordinary terms such as `lattice` and `ledger` are not codenames.
- Examples of the mapping ([#6832](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6832)):

  | Now                                                          | After                                                                      |
  | ------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
  | `folio`, `trait Folio`, `FolioValue`                         | `dump`, `trait Dump`, `DumpValue`                                          |
  | `FolioOp`, `FolioIf`, …                                      | `vize_l2::dump::{Op, If, …}`                                               |
  | `vize_impeto`                                                | `vize_l3`                                                                  |
  | `impeto.set-prop`                                            | `l3.set-prop`                                                              |
  | `davinci.s2_dom.*` counters                                  | `l2.dom.*`                                                                 |
  | `SpolveroFeed`, `inspector::spolvero`                        | `StageFeed`, `inspector::stages`                                           |
  | features `davinci-differential` / `davinci-dom-differential` | `legacy-differential` / `legacy-dom-differential`                          |
  | `davinci-opt`                                                | `vize dump` opt-in product compilation capture for raw DOM and SFC targets |

- Internal Davinci level crates have no external users yet, so their APIs, dump formats and serialized strings change without a compatibility period. Formerly published Cargo feature spellings stay as [temporary compatibility aliases](./2026-09-29-published-feature-compatibility.md) under the [support policy](../../release/support-policy.md). Published option literals in the L1→L2 emitter and legacy codegen remain patch compatible through the [SFC slot-scope carrier](./2026-09-29-no-slotted-api-compatibility.md). Legacy products keep strict output compatibility; the [#6898 SSR slot scope correction](./2026-09-27-ssr-slot-scope.md) adds its own regression corpus. Community Vapor [#7502](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/7502) escapes parser-decoded static attribute values before serializing runtime HTML templates, preserves the reporter input and quote/reference boundary cases in legacy compiler regressions, and verifies both public generated code and server-consumed IR templates without native-stage completion credit. Unchanged double-quoted source values stay verbatim; decoded, rewritten and quote-normalized values are escaped. Plain String and mapped EmitDocument sinks share the serializer, retaining source links without recording work in plain output and keeping all 100 pinned instruction ceilings unchanged. Community Vapor [#7570](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/7570) retains ordinary children beside named slot carriers as one implicit default slot, borrowing authored child runs without cloning the AST or lowering slot carriers twice. Reporter IR, public code, scope, ordering and named-only regressions cover the legacy correction without native-stage completion credit. Queue runtime conformance also proves Kanban, KanbanCard and KanbanColumn hydration now succeeds, so their three known-failure exceptions are removed; SSR, mount and the remaining hydration expectations stay enforced. Community Vapor [#7600](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/7600) shares the retained class merger with static style bindings, preserving static-first values in public transform_to_ir, whitespace-preserve compilation and recoverable parser lanes; reporter and boundary fixtures verify generated modules without native-stage completion credit. [The existing #7600 runtime regression](./2026-10-05-legacy-style-runtime.md) now reuses complete unchanged reporter fixture bytes in a source-built retained Vapor example for both whitespace modes and executes the original modules in fresh pinned rc.9/happy-dom children, checking static/dynamic style updates, cleanup, node identity, diagnostics and unmount; exact source Actions/protected delivery remain pending, happy-dom grants no Chromium/native/full-history credit, and publication hold remains active. Initial head8747 check-js reports one Node test-registration promise lint failure; await that registration without changing compiler/runtime assertions, pending fresh exact-head Actions. [Responsive edit diagnostics](./2026-10-06-responsive-edit-diagnostics.md) publishes current synchronous feedback before native waiting, preserves final ownership and reuses the same validated set; runtime and protected acceptance remain pending. Independent review strengthens the authored native-positive control to exact whole early/final packets without observed recapture. Actual Node import rejects constructor parameter properties; explicit equivalent fields repair only the gate harness and require fresh Actions. Current977 native controls pass, but Rust consumers expose unnecessary unversioned edit feedback; known source versions preserve old whole-wire observers while new exact-count laws retain every early/final packet. Private ef8 review also requires lint-enabled non-plain-script eligibility, preserving original native-only churn and single-complete queued-edit laws without waivers. Current b4 qualifies all19 whole gated publications and16,310 Rust tests while preserving original observers; a corrected audit retains one snapshot-release warning, with security/protected/installed acceptance still unfinished. Private installed-replay preparation extracts the original scenario in a move-only commit and requires actual GNU publication/driver/SDK authority, preserving all19 whole packets and four native gates; future identities stay null and no current installed or successor acceptance transfers. Actual security main48cb permits the same eight-source-commit preservation replay with24 owned non-doc blobs exact; fresh hosted union Actions and protected/installed acceptance remain mandatory ([paired actual-main decision](./2026-10-06-responsive-edit-diagnostics.md)).
- Product crates with art names (croquis, patina, glyph, maestro, canon, carton, …) keep their names. `docs/davinci/` keeps its name as the program name.
- The migration does not freeze other work. Move-only commits keep git rename detection working for in-flight fixes, renames are scripted (on a conflict, re-run the script on `main`), and PRs stay small. [MoonBit workflow naming](./2026-09-28-level-moonbit-workflow.md) records the bounded CI move for #6832; the remaining tooling names stay open. [L1 surface corpus naming](./2026-10-04-l1-surface-corpus-name.md) moves one feature-gated target with its original byte custody, preserves published aliases and historical witnesses, and requires actual feature-enabled PR and protected-queue execution of the unchanged 42-case battery without native product or history-gate credit. The same existing #7757 preserves its failed78>75 candidate while preparing the established nextest-discoverable standalone Vapor allocation-law harness: unchanged seven-case body/global counters/warm-up/caps and genuine spawned-worker ownership, strict CLI discovery/selection/assertion controls and narrowly retained full success output; old78 ownership remains UNKNOWN, and fresh source/full protected execution/all104×3/actual delivery are required before admission. The optional historical libtest-overlay recipe now refuses changed budget bytes before any archive/tool/process work; pure refusal laws and rejected thread arguments preserve its unavailable status pending separate protocol review, without altering the current automatic full Nextest gate. Initial fresh sourceacc correctly rejects only its generated Carton import coordinate18→21; refresh that exact field with all classifications/counts unchanged, retain the failed head and require fresh successor Actions. [L3 dump law naming](./2026-10-04-l3-dump-test-name.md) moves one native auto-discovered test target byte-for-byte, preserves its two round-trip laws and all historical/wire bytes, and requires fresh current-target Actions plus the full protected queue.

## Skeleton-first (2026-09-28)

Decided by the maintainer on 2026-09-28 ([#6826 comment](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6826#issuecomment-5857956448)).

- **The whole L0–L4 picture is laid down as a compiling skeleton first.** Unimplemented bodies are `todo!()` inside modules that start with `#![expect(clippy::todo, reason = "skeleton: #NNNN")]`; the workspace still denies `clippy::todo` and `#[allow]` everywhere else. Skeleton code is not reachable from any product path.
- **A CI ratchet counts those expectations per crate.** `tools/commands/ci/check-skeleton-todos.rs --check` (clippy job and `check:rust`) compares them with `tools/config/skeleton-todos.toml`; counts only go down, and a PR that fills a module lowers its line (`--write`).
- **Priority:** L0 (`vize_l0`, [#6833](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6833)), the L1 lexer (`vize_l1::markup`), L4 (`vize_l4`). **Completion audit (2026-10-03):** the [native ledger](../plan/completion-2026-10-03.md) records the immutable `bf3b6ee55883` source snapshot, 28 receipt-tracked actual deliveries, asynchronous owner/CI/queue distinctions and five unfinished default product replacements (0/5), with all five fix-history gates OPEN. Its permalink preserves the earlier f971 snapshot and source-qualified receipts; the [September 30 snapshot](../plan/completion-2026-09-30.md) remains historical. Older original JSX L2 and Handler L1 providers retain ownership priority. Issue closure requires native acceptance, terminal Actions and actual protected merge.
- **L0 ownership:** `vize_l0` owns the shared foundation, with `vize_carton` as its legacy facade ([directory decision](./2026-09-30-davinci-directory.md)). The [runtime decision](./2026-09-30-l0-runtime.md) moves Dump, keys, diagnostics, pass/fact managers and the derive into L0 and removes its skeletons. Exact legacy substrate re-exports last only until #6833 migrates consumer pages and imports and deletes the old crate. Platform/no-std isolation remains #6834. Allocation laws use a standalone executable so libtest reporting cannot pollute process-wide counters; the attached positive control stays exact. Observer defaults use canonical walk keys; replay rejects changed placeholders and partial integration before writing. The [native import decision](./2026-09-30-native-l0-imports.md) removes all native consumers from the old substrate graph. Runtime [#7283](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/pull/7283) is merged on fresh main with successful exact-head, strict instruction and protected-queue validation; Both consumer owner Stacks and retirement [#7298](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/pull/7298) have merged; #6833 is closed. The [summary owner decision](./2026-09-30-l2-summary-owner.md) moves existing interface summaries and their consumers directly to L2; cross-platform key laws follow those test owners. The [renderer owner decision](./2026-09-30-cli-renderer-owner.md) moves terminal rendering and all exact locale fixtures into the CLI. Croquis pages follow their [legacy owner](./2026-10-01-croquis-dump-owner.md); the inspector owns its [stage feed](./2026-10-01-inspector-feed-owner.md), and the inspection library owns [crash reports](./2026-10-01-inspection-repro-owner.md) and [legacy plan metadata](./2026-10-01-inspection-legacy-plans.md). The [foundation law decision](./2026-10-01-l0-runtime-laws.md) keeps all remaining runtime harnesses and fixed key fixtures under L0, with dev-only producer oracles. The [substrate retirement decision](./2026-10-01-runtime-substrate-retirement.md) migrates the last legacy contracts and benchmarks and removes the reviewed compatibility package, merged as `d457f95dbc9c079c73919a258e0bbbb1cfe24e48` with successful [queue Check](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/actions/runs/36790341706). The plugin host identity hashes the actual L0 and derive manifests and sources, including dirty edits; replay replaces the retired root and missing inputs still fail builds. Four moved benchmark source identities are rebound from three identical [Actions measurements](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/actions/runs/36778058361); all 100 numeric ceilings, windows and methodologies remain unchanged and passed. Inspection owners and foundation laws ([#7290](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/pull/7290) through [#7293](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/pull/7293)) are actually merged on fresh main, with successful exact-head and final protected-queue checks; the final package retirement follows them. The [host runtime boundary](./2026-10-01-host-runtime-boundary.md) moves Corsa executable discovery and transport classification back to Carton, including their existing laws and target-only `which` edge. Legacy callers use that real owner; native storage imports and the strict reverse-dependency gate stay in L0. Config, i18n, profiler and portable protocols remain unfinished under #6834. The same boundary corrects #6832’s stale stage catalogue to the actual L0 owner and includes the existing L4 package without claiming its emitters complete; Carton inventory references remain visible as legacy infrastructure, with no native-stage credit. Replay preserves complete aliased/nested grouped imports and rejects unmigrated host roots; actual Rust compilations and deliberately-unmigrated consumers verify this contract. Replay and checking share an offset-preserving Rust lexical view so comment/string examples remain data; every split import retains its original conditional attributes, tested with disabled imports and literal fixtures. The maintainer requires TypeScript or Rust Script tooling; this replay and its seven Rust-compilation/integration laws now run directly in TypeScript, without added Python files. The first host-boundary queue candidate exceeded the unchanged Croquis full/stress-wide ceiling by 101 instructions identically across three executions and was dequeued. Classify directives once before setup-read and optional usage handling, preserving order and output; validate all 100 pinned measurements before requeueing. The relocation regression cause remains unestablished, and no budgets or measurement contracts are relaxed. The [caller-owned profile-input plan](./2026-10-04-profile-input-isolation-plan.md) records the real Curator duration-only export’s remaining native global-tracking/lock coupling and records the private provider plus genuine Curator consumer using existing owned span/counter inputs for the sole host serializer; complete telemetry, null unavailable observations, original whole JSON, lazy marks and all104 caps remain exact. Draft native Stack #7814 (#7812 → #7813) is source-validating. Its first exact child passed the four new provider laws and real allocator-session law but failed stale replay/suffix-only tooling witnesses; the paired correction retains whole wire oracles and exact historical allocator-replay bytes. Fresh source Actions and protected actual merges remain pending; this does not complete host isolation. The current-main provider and genuine consumer deliveries retain all original source/law bytes and incoming decisions, record verified reporter attribution without rewriting history, and require fresh exact-head source and protected native Stack proof before actual merge. The first source-qualified candidates omit merge-commit coauthor trailers in their actual squash messages; remove the prefix and retain meaningful regular qualification records with verified reporter trailers before fresh native Stack re-entry. Both actual regular layer records retain authenticated four-provider-law, real consumer-session and 23-preserved-law execution custody; inspect each new protected squash footer before accepting signed merge.
- **Queue order for #7043:** the maintainer's explicit queue entry permits this validated L0 leaf slice before #6832 closes; the bounded exception and resume checks are recorded in [the L0 leaf queue decision](./2026-09-28-l0-leaf-queue-order.md). Its independent instruction-count run remains a merge prerequisite with unchanged ceilings. Three broad SSR allocation experiments failed; the original emitter on current `main` missed only SSR medium +108 and large +4. A narrow capacity reserve for ordinary default-slot content with one to four children and a Clippy-safe `first()` guard skips unused child-end scans for named and dynamic slots; exact-head strict and ordinary CI passed. The first queue entry was removed for a prefix `Cargo.lock` conflict; synchronize after preceding entries land, then revalidate and requeue. Scope audit: L0 re-exports Carton's storage and the PR does not switch any live compiler product path; #6880 still gates such a switch.
- **"Lightweight Davinci" means runtime cost and process weight.** Runtime: instruction counts, zero-cost unobserved paths, no reparses or serialization between levels ([#6868](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6868), [#6869](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6869)). Process: no committed whole-repo ledgers, shorter PR-tier CI, short PR bodies and issue comments, no hash- or evidence-heavy verifiers where a normal test does.
- **The dump/naming stack #6906 → #6907 → #6943 → #6944 → #6945 → #6946 → #6947 is collapsed into one PR** on `main`; the seven PRs are closed in its favour. **Parallel structural work (2026-09-28):** the maintainer [approved](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6826#issuecomment-5871526916) independently reviewable #6832 and L1–L4/dialect structural PRs opening and merging in parallel. The [order table](./2026-09-27-level-restructure-order.md) gates real provider APIs, not whole-issue closure: #6833 may delete `vize_davinci` after #6832 retires `davinci-opt` and hands off capture/Dump contracts, and #6833 moves active imports to level crates; it need not wait for #6832 closure. #6832 stays OPEN until its full naming, serialized-wire, CLI and product-capture scope and the crate-name audit are complete. Product fix-history gates remain unchanged. Dependent code PRs register as native GitHub Stacks and enter the protected queue by exact-head-green prefixes.

## Level dump naming

The L3 package identity, stage feed types, level dump API, versioned dump protocols and `vize dump --roundtrip` are recorded in [level dump naming](./2026-09-28-level-dump-naming.md). The inspector's native stage ladder is a separate run; [production stage capture](./2026-09-28-production-stage-capture.md) observes same-run DOM/SSR/Vapor/SFC stages, effective options and the final selected lane after source-map parity. Direct ordinary dispatch and Vapor inlining preserve instruction budgets. The bounded Vue splitter handles nested JS braces, comments, recognizable regex, Vue+TS postfix `!` and JS postfix `++`/`--` division; unsupported nested template expressions remain uncertain. CLI capture uses real SFC filenames, rejects unresolved external blocks, and preserves user-modified dump files. Build `--dump-dir` observes the product compile and records its complete versioned feed with owner-checked reuse, truthful Vue parser syntax labels and unavailable external blocks. Playground classifies observed `(level, step)` pages, shows L4 emit separately from later SFC module assembly, and isolates target failures; the WASM result's separate legacy template compile remains an unresolved #6832 follow-up. [Host retirement](./2026-09-29-opt-host-retirement.md) removes the old no-op binary after typed test migration while #6833 owns the separate L0 Dump substrate. CLI, Build and Playground capture have merged; #6832 remains open. The [fallible capture decision](./2026-10-02-fallible-stage-capture.md) retains lazy typed inspection failures beside valid pages and the same final-selection discard policy, with its laws discovered through ordinary Rust child modules; native L2 dump and host-feed integration remain separate follow-ups.
For [#6832](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6832), L2's 13 auto-discovered `folio_*` test targets move to `dump_*` in a move-only commit; source references and live documentation links follow in a separate commit, while `.folio` fixture bytes and extensions remain unchanged. [L2 span support naming](./2026-10-04-l2-span-support-name.md) moves one private span-oracle module byte-for-byte, updates only its module references, and preserves every law, diagnostic and fixture byte with unchanged dependency declarations. [L0 dump law names](./2026-10-04-l0-dump-test-names.md) move two auto-discovered targets byte-for-byte, update current links and replay destinations, and preserve original source names and all captured strings; fresh current-target Actions and the full protected queue are required. [Playground stage view naming](./2026-09-28-playground-stage-view-names.md) records the active source/e2e renames and still-open UI/DOM inventory. [L1→L2 provenance profile keys](./2026-09-28-l1-to-l2-profile-wire.md) record the native profile wire mapping. The existing #7213 residual [published feature repair](./2026-09-29-published-feature-compatibility.md#existing-pr-7213-residual-repair) preserves old and new SSR benchmark members, ports the existing renamer to TypeScript, and rejects missing or duplicate protected blocks/members before any write; complete post-rewrite Cargo feature witnesses replace the weak inherited assertion, with same-PR actual Actions and protected merge required. The final own-only residual candidate preserves accepted signed7f and its actually merged original Program/zero-Vue incoming source; the existing7213 head guard, fresh Actions and protected terminal merge remain required. After actual SFC #7497 merged as signed21f, the same #7213 repair preserves its normal L2 edge and complete incoming source; only the generated SFC shard conflicted, and the unchanged scanner derives the union with all375 data rows and fourteen manifest coordinate shifts. Current-head Actions and protected merge remain required. The maintainer [closed #7220 as superseded](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/pull/7220#issuecomment-5966675899) at 2026-10-03 07:15:04 UTC without merging or changing its dd127028 source. Its retained witness finding is corrected in the same #7213 compatibility slice: the direct default callback is asserted separately and reset before an original interpolation is tokenized, so the final counter proves a real tokenizer event. Published baseline/current consumer and release execution remain distinct pending gates. Exact313 Actions pass the feature and baseline tooling laws; its release-only SemVer consumer job was skipped, so actual hosted external tokenizer consumer execution remains unfinished. The signed21f composition retains the same tokenization witness without transferring prior runtime or merge credit. The same existing #7213 decision and all retained tokenizer qualifications now share this owned paragraph, preserving every incoming tokenizer/container paragraph unchanged so independent adjacent style decisions do not conflict. Exact5392 configured checks and genuine100 passed; this prose-only successor preserves all functional sources but needs fresh configured Actions, instruction and protected merge, without transferring old green or external-consumer credit.

## Rust verification for L3 (2026-09-28)

The maintainer chose Rust Kani for Davinci verification and closed the Lean namespace rename PR [#7052](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/pull/7052). New L3 properties use Kani harnesses against production Rust source, starting with the class and effect lattice in `tests/formal/kani/l3_lattice.rs`; each harness states its finite input domain. Bounded verification does not establish an unbounded theorem. Keep differential fixtures and Lean checks until each claim has replacement coverage, then remove the old lane separately. Remaining naming and shared CLI/playground production capture keep [#6832](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6832) open.

## `vize_davinci` is deleted

Tracked in [#6833](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6833) and [#6834](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6834).

- **`vize_l0`** is the foundation level. It holds source, arena, span, ids (`NodeId`, `AnalysisId`), side tables, artifact keys, the dump trait and runtime, diagnostics and witnesses, the pass and fact managers, and the level registry. It is carved out of `vize_carton`, which keeps config, i18n, LSP, resolver and profiler code.
- Some code moves to the crate that uses it:
  - `summary` → `vize_l2::summary`
  - `render` → the `vize` CLI
  - the Croquis dump page → croquis
  - the stage feed → curator
  - the repro page → the CLI
  - `legacy_plan` → test support
- The accepted trade-off is that `vize_l0` becomes large. It stays readable through its modules.

## Dependency direction

Tracked in [#6831](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6831) and [#6851](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6851).

- `crates/` may consume `davinci/`; `davinci/` cannot consume `crates/` through normal or build dependencies, even optionally, on another target, or through a workspace helper. Dev-only differential oracles remain permitted. The directory gate has no exceptions. Shared Namespace, error-code and stage identities move below Relief, whose existing public paths re-export them; the old level-to-Relief allowlist is empty. The [UTF-16 coordinate decision](./2026-10-01-utf16-coordinate-owner.md) makes line_index own Position/Range and keeps the old host path as an exact re-export; UTF-16 behavior and serialized coordinate bytes remain unchanged. The dependent host-module move follows this provider. The [LSP host decision](./2026-10-01-lsp-host-owner.md) moves existing protocol records, framing, counters and classification into Carton and re-exports L0 coordinates without conversion. Replay preserves the fixed message snapshot and native-only resolver dependencies; config, i18n, profiler and full platform isolation remain open. The [config document provider](./2026-10-01-config-document-provider.md) owns the existing in-memory projections behind an opaque raw representation; its loader consumes the provider before the dependent Carton host move. Typed values, aliases, ordered rules and errors retain their existing contract without an extra parse or serialization stage; this bounded split keeps #6834 open. The [config host decision](./2026-10-01-config-host-owner.md) moves discovery, file/Node/PKL evaluation and path-bearing loaded records into Carton, whose real CLI/LSP callers consume the exact L0 document types. Replay keeps error/snapshot bytes and all numeric ceilings unchanged and drops L0's unused PKL runtime dependency; effective-type placement, path matching/project context, i18n, profiler and full platform isolation remain open. The [project host decision](./2026-10-01-project-host-owner.md) moves invocation/config/tsconfig path selection and its existing tests unchanged into Carton; the CLI facade and two Maestro callers consume that host model while TypeCheckerConfig stays in L0. Relative-path precedence, explicit override, absolute/unrooted identity, output and numeric ceilings are preserved; path matching, framework model placement, i18n, profiler and full platform isolation remain open. The [config matcher host decision](./2026-10-01-config-matcher-host-owner.md) moves ordered glob/path matching unchanged into Carton with its optional globset dependency and lint-glob feature; actual CLI lint-plan/file-collection and LSP scopes use that host owner. Rule-plan values and VueVersion remain available in L0 for their existing consumers, while ordered negation, escapes, path normalization, warning bytes and ceilings keep their contract; framework placement, i18n, profiler and full platform isolation remain open. The [message lookup provider](./2026-10-01-message-lookup-provider.md) introduces caller-supplied compiler localization with unchanged translator fallback and substitution; catalog/global storage ownership remains a separate #6834 slice. The [i18n host decision](./2026-10-01-i18n-host-owner.md) moves the embedded translator, locales, global catalog and unchanged payloads into Carton; actual CLI/Relief callers bind the portable MessageLookup while Patina/Canon/Vitrine retain their host locale behavior. Ordered registration, locale aliases, fallback, unknown-key ownership, substitutions, diagnostic text and ceilings stay unchanged without an extra pipeline stage or L0 forwarding path. Exact import and replay laws retain storage/dependency enforcement; framework placement, profiler and full platform isolation remain open. The [profiler readback provider](./2026-10-02-profile-readback-provider.md) exposes the existing owned span/counter reads with identical bodies, clone counts, allocation pauses and bucket ordering, for the dependent host JSON export; clocks, collection, global state and full platform isolation remain open. The [profile export host decision](./2026-10-02-profile-export-host-owner.md) moves the existing JSON exporter and wire laws unchanged into Carton; actual CLI, Curator and SFC attribution callers consume the same L0 metrics through the public readbacks. Schema/JSON bytes, caps, ranking, package version and allocation counters stay exact, with no extra readback/stage and only dev host oracles below Carton. The SFC manifest law permits the reviewed dev host edge and rejects normal/build Carton edges, including aliases and targets; collector clocks/global state, framework placement and full platform isolation remain open. The [host allocator selection decision](./2026-10-02-profile-allocator-selection.md) removes the concrete System default and constructors from the existing generic L0 accounting wrapper. Carton supplies the exact System-backed value to the allocation-budget/export binaries; unchanged mimalloc consumers retain their caller-selected backend. All allocator hooks/counters, fixtures and ceilings stay exact, with no additional wrapper or dependency. The preferred-L0 SFC gate admits only the exact test System factory initializer with its `#[global_allocator]` attribute, rejects Carton storage and other calls, and is included with the exporter exact-byte contract in zero-write replay preflight. Thread-local suppression/totals, clocks, locks, arena/recursion facilities and full platform isolation remain unfinished. The private [caller-owned fused-walk timing provider](./2026-10-04-portable-walk-timing-plan.md) replaces the native observer’s existing open state with caller marks over the same Timer and its genuine Curator child reuses existing host samples without another clock read or walk mirror; six owned-mark laws and two actual Curator failure/restart controls await Actions, while full clock/platform isolation and product completion stay unfinished. The [remaining dependency audit](../plan/remaining-providers-2026-10-04.md) pins current actual routes and live history gates without transferring older snapshot counts.
- Level crates never take normal dependencies on legacy crates: `vize_armature`, `vize_relief`, `vize_atelier_*`, `vize_croquis`, `vize_croquis_cf`. Legacy may depend on levels. Dev-dependencies used as differential oracles are fine.
- **Croquis counts as legacy.** Script analysis is rebuilt natively in the levels ([#6844](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6844)). No adapter presents legacy output as Davinci facts.
- The [declaration ratchet](./2026-09-27-foundation-stack-replay.md#dependency-gate) records shrinking #6831 permissions, enforcement and remaining scope.

## L1: what the text _is_

Tracked in [#6835](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6835), [#6836](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6836) and [#6837](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6837). The detailed design is in the [#6836 design comment](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6836#issuecomment-5847794929).

[Authored formatter tag forms](./2026-09-27-formatter-tag-forms.md) records #6846's resolution-free audit and public byte corpus; source-built Actions and the actual queue remain required before closing the issue. [Expression wrapper custody](2026-10-05-formatter-expression-wrapper.md) records #7923: use the existing sole unary argument AST to preserve grouped calls/members and consumer-required sequence parentheses, retain the arena/buffer/single formatter stage, and require twenty-two whole SFC fixed points plus original/after official DOM, click and SSR runtime evidence without changing history or caps. Its strict current script owner hash follows the wrapper file while all original pins, revisions, retained law bodies and expected bytes remain unchanged. The converse bare-sequence consumer keeps original argument bytes; only parser-span-proven authored outer groups retain printed parentheses, excluding stored comments. Six inverse runtime controls extend the unchanged original sixteen rows, and their successor needs fresh source qualification. The actual 8f protected-prefix replay retains every incoming owner and owned source byte; fresh exact-head source/protected reports remain required. The actual 0034 BOM/main replay retains every incoming source owner, original author/trailer and all original vectors; fresh hosted/protected qualification and release remain required. The [bare sequence directive continuation decision](./2026-10-05-formatter-sequence-directive-indent.md) repairs the four hard42a Matrix drifts by carrying the retained AST branch tag into existing attribute reanchoring; all four whole pinned originals, complete current format1/2/3/diagnostics and forty-nine independent DOM/click/SSR controls qualify the repair, while all original22/300 bytes and ceilings stay exact and historical snippets receive no whole-output credit. Fresh protected delivery and a complete finite release Matrix remain required.

- **Boundary rule:** L1 is what the text _is_ (the concrete syntax of every grammar, lossless). L2 is what it _does_.
- **Markup** is a grammar × profile matrix:
  - Grammars: Vue now; Svelte, Angular and others later.
  - Profiles: `document` (HTML and in-DOM rules, used by petite-vue) and `component` (SFC template rules).
  - One shared lexer, `Lexer<P: Profile>`, with static dispatch.
  - The template tokenizer moves from armature into `vize_l1::markup`, so armature depends on L1 and not the other way round. [#7136's instruction gate](./2026-09-29-l1-tokenizer-instruction-gate.md) passed all 100 pinned probes on #7152 main after bounded escape-scanning optimizations; #7155 then changed only npm/oxlint files, so exact PR Actions and the protected queue must validate the rebased head, and #6835 stays open.
- The [L1 tokenizer ownership record](./2026-09-28-l1-markup-skeleton.md#tokenizer-source-relocation-6835) tracks the source move and dependency inversion separately from the remaining generic-profile parity work. The #6831 allowlist removes the now-stale L1→Armature permission for L1 and L1→L2; The directory split removes the final L1→Relief permission. Moving the same tokenizer code does not switch a product from its legacy parser path; that still requires #6880. The [2026-10-01 default-provider slice](./2026-10-01-l1-default-markup-provider.md) exposes the existing profile-generic lexer and full entity decoder in ordinary L1 builds, removes two panicking skeletons, and keeps the callback adapter feature-gated. Downstream integration laws exercise the ordinary library build; production parsing remains on the preserved tokenizer. The prior thin-LTO regression makes all 100 unchanged exact-head instruction ceilings a prerequisite before queueing; #6835 and #6880 remain open. The [native surface construction decision](./2026-10-01-l1-native-surface-construction.md) adds ordinary-build Component entry points whose direct native Sink feeds the existing recovery and authored-projection builder. Complete entity values record one authored span; fixed delimiters and narrow options exclude unsupported Document/raw modes. Root parse and product routes stay unchanged, and v-pre, full profiles and typed embeds remain unfinished. Exact event/tree/diagnostic laws and unchanged all-100 Actions gates precede native Stack queueing. The [standalone L1 roundtrip decision](./2026-10-01-native-l1-roundtrip.md) connects only vize dump --level l1 --roundtrip to that provider. All-level capture continues to observe the selected product's actual stages; compiler, ICE and reducer routes remain unchanged. The [selected original root-text provider](./2026-10-03-selected-root-text-condense.md) adds a sealed default-Vue-3-condense source/profile receipt from the same genuine selected root child, with O(1) original neighbor/ordinal joins, complete authored spans, zero-allocation identity/omission/single-space values and arena-only changed mixed text. Original ASCII-only/drop rules preserve Unicode and reject entity/nested/foreign/reported-recovery families; no second parse/tree walk, existing preserve-family change or whole Component/File/product completion is introduced. Its genuinely dependent L2 root cursor receiver and complete module/runtime/map closure follow in a native Stack; fresh exact-head source Actions, protected complete suites/all-100 and actual merge remain required. The protected queue baseline refresh follows the actually merged census policy: preserve deletion of grep-only shards, regenerate the canonical 20-file inventory and complete fresh source-qualified reports, keep authoritative resolved/nonproduct tables, and rerun exact-head Actions before ready Stack admission. The paired issue receipts record the unexpected unmergeable queue appearance, immediate absent-entry query and temporary draft containment without inferring its cause.
- The [single markup machine decision](./2026-10-01-l1-single-markup-machine.md) retires the duplicate tokenizer state machine. The published `Tokenizer` facade owns the default-build generic lexer and `CompatSink`; first-scalar entities, all diagnostic and quote mappings, live callback mode, custom delimiters, repeated EOF and state-preserving profile switches retain their compatibility contracts. Independent frozen original-machine callback and complete parser AST/diagnostic records localize 10,767 cases; adapter-versus-facade tests now establish consistency only. Existing product parser routes remain, and exact-head output and all 100 unchanged instruction gates precede queueing. Native v-pre scope control, Document tree rules, full surface admission and typed embeds remain unfinished; #6835 stays open. The initial exact-head run exceeded two pinned ceilings. Retaining the private state-number gap reduces their cost but still exceeds both limits; interpolation now skips callback-free bytes through the existing bounded byte loop, with all 100 unchanged ceilings and frozen contracts required again. The run loop holds the immutable input slice once while retaining cursor writes and live callback mode.
- **Container** is the file-format layer. SFC block splitting moves here out of Croquis, laid out so Svelte, Analog and TSRX containers fit later. The [original Vue descriptor provider](./2026-10-02-l1-vue-descriptor-observation.md) classifies real blocks at the same emission loop and privately lends source-checked JS/TS Module roles with complete original captures/errors; public Container records cannot grant admission. Setup-only TS and reversed authored order retain genuine indices and whole-root spans. Explicit Vue 3 options, raw attribute/language agreement and typed unsupported boundaries limit this structural family; real Program/File/factory integration, full dialects/styles/custom blocks and every product gate remain unfinished. Native CSS/v-bind facts and output, scoped/module/preprocessor semantics and whole-product gates remain unfinished. Actual qualified-rule/declaration callbacks, actual selector/value tokens, parser errors and absolute source spans remain owned beside the complete original block. Only one complete simple-class rule with flat values can lend the private scoped insertion receipt; no copied stylesheet, normalized AST or legacy helper supplies authority. Actual quoted tokens containing binding spellings or comment markers refuse because pinned Vue can derive CSS variables from those values; original token spans/partial declarations survive and no raw stylesheet scan supplies authority. The native scoped CSS/whole-module consumer and runtime acceptance remain unfinished until its dependent slice passes protected gates. Validated full scope IDs attach the same __scopeId to the complete L4 module; default filename hashing is independently checked against ordinary scoped CSS as a dev-only oracle. Separate exact whole ordinaryCss goldens retain its selector-whitespace trimming, while native/pinned Vue CSS preserves every authored byte around the scope insertion. A dedicated protected tooling-shard-1 action captures the current Rust complete modules/CSS/maps and mounts those exact modules through real Vue, retaining source/runtime receipts. Source-bound complete module/CSS fixtures, Unicode/CRLF/full-source map laws and pinned actual Vue DOM selector/update/unmount laws cover the bounded family. Broader selectors/values, nested rules, binding/Modules/preprocessor semantics and whole-product/history gates remain unfinished; hosted exact-head and protected queue acceptance are required. The [class-list receipt decision](./2026-10-03-l1-scoped-class-lists.md) prepares a private borrowed proof over the same actual rule tokens for comma-separated literal simple classes. Pre-comma whitespace, comment/escape normalization and mixed selectors refuse; original parse/source/custody and the single-class API remain unchanged. Complete consumer/runtime/source/protected delivery remains unfinished, and this private provider is not acceptance. The [native scoped class-list decision](./2026-10-03-native-scoped-class-list-css.md) prepares a dependent consumer of every original parser-proven class-token insertion without another parse or offset collection. Three complete original-SFC/module/CSS/map fixtures and each independent real Vue selector alternative cover preserved Unicode/CRLF bytes, actual computed CSS, outside exclusion and default setup identity. Its protected scoped action requires fresh Rust captures for both single-class and list families. Normalization-dependent shapes and broader CSS remain typed refusals; private publication, exact-source and protected acceptance remain unfinished. The [literal scoped empty decision](./2026-10-04-native-scoped-empty-css.md) prepares one complete native `.class:empty` slice from a private borrowed fixed window of the same actual classname/colon/pseudo tokens. The consumer inserts only at the original classname token end and preserves every other emitted CSS byte/map, accepted class/list family and complete SFC/module custody. Three whole-source/module/CSS fixtures, pinned Vue full CSS/maps and actual Chromium empty/nonempty/outside/setup/unmount laws cover the bounded family; the guaranteed protected scoped action requires fresh Rust captures before browser acceptance. Escaped/uppercase/mixed/comment-dependent pseudos and broader CSS remain typed refusals; source Actions, protected full/all-100 and actual merge remain required. Its initial #7650 candidate e9d1161f passed guaranteed fresh scoped Rust/Chromium capture but the global JS job failed before build/history on pinned Wild 0.9.0 release-asset HTTP 503 after all retries; the known-red entry was actually removed. Existing Scriptless/Setup/source-class/style gates remain, generated CSS/ScopeId stay above L2, and unsupported original CSS/parser custody survives without partial template construction. Private source and authored laws are not styled SSR acceptance; independent constructor review, actual common prerequisite, genuine consumer/module/CSS/maps/hydration, hosted source and protected full/all-100/capture/merge remain required. The [native selected scoped SSR decision](./2026-10-04-native-selected-scoped-ssr.md) prepares the genuine dependent same-File SSR receipt and complete original CSS/module/maps consumer. The same validated compilation-option scope feeds existing CSS output, every original SSR opening in its existing encoder loop and component metadata; old unstyled entries retain StyledSource and None scope. Three whole no-authored-class SFC contracts, independent pinned full CSS/SSR/client maps and fresh native server/Chromium hydration/node-reuse/empty/outside/unmount laws preserve every authored byte and leave generated scope unlinked. The scoped action requires fresh source-conditional and unconditional protected Rust SSR captures without fixture fallback, retaining all accepted CSS families; a disjoint predicate joins the existing SSR flag and first-worker tooling fallback without changing old source predicates or workflow length. Exact original CSS-body intervals reject coherent equal-byte outside origins, and complete source/module/CSS/raw-map hashes join actual server/browser packets; both whole maps/raw strings are frozen from genuine provisional capture and compared unconditionally before admission. Provider #7674 actually merged signed 47799777 after protected Check 37166463706; consumer 7e34 source Check 37165968762 passed after one unchanged-source Docker-only job rerun, and its literal-main 6a3 replay still requires fresh source and protected acceptance. Broader styles/setup/DOM/Vapor/default/history remain unfinished; private source and stock reference success are not native acceptance, which still needs review, native Stack, fresh Actions, protected full/all-100/captures and signed actual merge. The [original outer-template frame decision](./2026-10-04-original-template-frame-names.md) retains actual closing-name geometry only at the sole successful closing match, forwards the depth-zero template event and stores a scalar pair on the existing private template role. TemplateView.frame_names borrows the actual Descriptor/selection and whole-root authored spans; foreign equal-buffer parses and caller captures cannot grant membership. Public captures, script/style roles, original case/gap/self-close/missing/uncertainty policies and all104 ceilings stay exact, without a new scanner, parse, walk, index, allocation or stage. Seventeen runtime and six lifetime/private-constructor compile-fail laws plus a positive control require independent review, exact-source Actions and protected actual merge. Maestro outer-frame/full-session history and default products remain separate unfinished work.
- **Dialect syntax hooks** decompose directive names (`v-on:click.stop`, `@click`, `#default`, `:[dyn]`). They play the role of MLIR custom assembly formats.
- **Typed embeds.** Attribute values, mustaches and dynamic arguments become `Embed { grammar, source }` with `Grammar = Shape × Lang`:
  - `Shape` is the role inside the markup: `Expr`, `HandlerBody`, `ForHead`, `SlotParams`, `FilterChain`, …
  - `Lang` is the host language, resolved once per file: JS and TS now; Flow, MoonBit and others later.
  - Composite shapes (`ForHead`, `FilterChain`) are built by the dialect from language pieces.
- **Embed trees** are a separate L1 artifact keyed by embed id: [The original strict literal receipt](./2026-10-03-original-strict-literal-receipt.md) retains legacy numeric/string spelling facts during the existing lexer decode and both checkpoint paths, without changing ordinary ASTs or syntax diagnostics. It is a provider for authentic native setup eligibility; general semantics, consumer acceptance and TS erasure remain separate. [The original expression literal receipt](./2026-10-03-original-expression-literal-receipt.md) exposes the existing stock lexer scalar through the complete original admitted Expression owner, preserving syntax admission, comments, diagnostics and committed checkpoint facts without another parse, AST/source scan, stored flag or stage. Four Descriptor-selected JS/TS interpolation laws cover legacy/modern spellings, comment/raw controls, entity decode, operand moves and syntax-hole refusal. False does not certify strict-module semantics; same-File interpolation association, sole-L3 target consumption and complete captured module/map/runtime closure remain unfinished. Fresh hosted exact-head Actions and protected full/instruction terminal merge decide delivery. The [original attribute-head provider](./2026-10-04-original-attribute-head-provider.md) retains the actual short selected/attribute/name frame and one dialect decomposition, reused by the unchanged conditional preparation tail without a second head analysis; original refusal order, maps/profiles and existing observer controls remain. Exact hosted/protected/literal acceptance and a genuine whole conditional formatter consumer remain pending; no L2 File, target or default authority is granted.
  - The JS/TS tree is the oxc AST plus spans; the source bytes are authoritative.
  - A broken expression becomes a hole in its own tree and never breaks the markup tree.
  - Embedding works in both directions and nests (markup inside script for JSX, TSRX and Angular inline templates).
- **Entity decoding** happens when an embed is built. The embed carries the decoded text and a decode map, so language providers never see HTML.
- **`v-pre`** is handled in L1, because it switches the lexing mode of its children.
- The #6836 native `v-pre` child compares the Armature parser's complete AST and diagnostics against the L1 lexer plus compatibility callback adapter in both component and document profiles. The compiler still uses its old production tokenizer until #6880. [Its held-branch handoff](./2026-09-29-l1-v-pre-resume.md) records why draft #7139 is closed while #6835/#6841 remain open and the exact conditions for publication and merge.
- L1's surface recorder and builder share HTML namespace and interactive-element implicit-close rules. A nested HTML anchor or button closes its prior `v-pre` lexical scope at the same start tag as tree recovery; SVG anchors retain authored nesting. The native lexer adapter uses the parser's live callback mode for the same boundary. SSR keeps nonempty `v-pre` outlet fallbacks on its legacy route until #6880, including when L1 correctly classifies a mustache as text. The [v-pre whitespace-plan correction](./2026-10-05-v-pre-whitespace-plan.md) forwards the existing removal plan into literal text lowering, retains exact dropped-byte provenance and the complete original upstream witness, and requires whole native/legacy DOM and SSR results plus protected delivery without comparator or corpus waivers (#7939). Its DOM and SSR test/dev inventory rows are both regenerated from actual native capture imports; the omitted first-publication DOM row is corrected before acceptance. First actual1c source is red on fused-literal leading whitespace, invalid authored self-closing controls and one storage census occurrence; its bounded successor preserves original228B/whole comparisons and defers Drop until the existing complete run boundary.
- **Language providers** are bundles of optional capabilities, one per level (like MLIR interfaces):
  - L1 syntax entry points
  - L2 semantics (today's `ExprDialect`)
  - L4 emission, the type-check projection and the checker host
  - the formatter printer

  A missing capability falls back to the opaque path or a diagnostic. There is one dynamic dispatch per file; everything after it is static. Template expressions follow `<script lang>`, and a language mismatch between `<script>` and `<script setup>` is a diagnostic.

- The [markup and container record](./2026-09-28-l1-markup-skeleton.md) tracks the `Profile`, `Sink`, lex error and directive-hook contracts: L1 surface parsing retains its published tokenizer facade over the single default-build generic lexer and callback adapter; compiler consumers retain their existing parser routes and product admission gates. The moved tokenizer adds storage rows, regenerates the v-on corpus and publishes L1 before Armature; source and decision-record length gates retain their limits. The [original Document lexical owner](./2026-10-03-native-document-lexical-owner.md) runs the actual Document profile once over a checked complete original root, retaining authored declarations, all callback coordinates and lexical diagnostics behind sealed immutable views. Only a normal full lexical run with the actual terminal-state receipt can mint its owner-bound completion; recovered syntax, silent pending EOF states, unfinished declarations and inherited skipped declaration windows refuse. Sticky driver/sink facts make readback constant-time and distinguish recovered windows from exact declaration frames. The [native explicit-envelope HTML structure](./2026-10-03-native-document-html-structure.md) directly consumes that sole original event tape and private original arena into sealed element ancestry, with actual ASCII name folding, true void insertion and ignored nonvoid slashes. Exact authored html/head/body modes and original closing frames refuse implicit, recovered, Vue-flavored and unsupported browser grammar; the same eight original law sources bind native structure to independent Chromium DOMParser ancestry. This bounded element-only subset certifies no text/attribute DOM values or general profile/product completion. The [original Document entity values](./2026-10-04-original-document-entity-values.md) retain the sole native callback expansion and authored span in the same private event, including corrected numeric and multiscalar named references; immutable readback performs no second decode or allocation. Existing bounded envelope construction admits only actual TAB/LF/FF/CR/SPACE entity expansions outside body, with Unicode/nonspace and once-decoded literal refusals retained. The eight unchanged plus four new whole-source census rows require native and actual Chromium ancestry/no-quirks agreement; source/full Actions and protected unchanged instruction checks with actual merge remain required. General DOM values, petite-vue scope/effect/L2 and product/history/default completion remain unfinished. The [original Document static attributes](./2026-10-04-original-document-static-attributes.md) borrow actual original start-tag callback ranges into sealed element-bound receipts, retaining authored name/content/quote geometry and streaming HTML ASCII-folded first-duplicate names plus once-decoded values. ## Multi-framework

Tracked in [#6829](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6829) and [#6855](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6855)–[#6860](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6860).

- This supersedes charter row #1. Other frameworks are in-tree and include the compiler, with parity against each reference compiler.
- The order is **TSRX → Solid → others** (Svelte, Angular/Analog).
- Five independent axes: container, markup, profile, lang, framework. [The #6855 neutral-axis design](./2026-10-04-l1-neutral-axes.md) assigns module ownership, explicit compositions and syntax-only hooks; registry/providers and native product acceptance remain separate.
- L2 keeps neutral core ops plus framework dialect ops.
- L3 gets a neutral reactivity vocabulary (signal, derived, effect, ordering). [The #6857 vocabulary review](./2026-10-04-l3-neutral-vocabulary.md) retains the demand split and assigns current flat kinds to a typed Vapor dialect; neutral/lattice/ordering contracts are design destinations.
- L4 gets one target per framework runtime.

## Vue Fes Japan (2026-10-24)

- **In scope:** Vue (templates and every Vue dialect), JS/TS, JSX/TSX.
- **TSRX:** not a priority; nice to have if something runs.
- **Other frameworks:** only the neutral types and names are designed before the talk. Flow is not in scope at all.
- **Goal (A):** for the in-scope inputs, all five products run on the shared L1/L2 structure as far as native work goes, with per-product native-only acceptance rates.
- **Deletion (B)** follows later, under the criteria above; the fail-closed [readiness audit and crate-removal guard](./2026-09-28-deletion-readiness.md) record #6854's product, tier, dialect, project and stability blockers; route swaps remain unguarded.
- Unfinished work is reported as unfinished. There are no legacy-backed shortcuts. [The October delivery checkpoints](./2026-10-01-native-delivery-checkpoints.md) set October 10/17 targets and October 18–23 stabilization, without a deadline guarantee. The [October 4 delivery instruction](./2026-10-04-delivery-control.md) prioritizes community reports, actual PR/Stack/queue delivery, frequent existing-product releases and a measured 10x typechecker target; preserving source commit messages in squash merges retains explicit reporter attribution through the protected queue.

## Performance

The toolchain aims to be extremely fast. The layering must not add pipelines or serialization cost. Native attribute helper ownership #8009 adds only the missing native cfg for its sole gated caller; [the feature record](./2026-10-06-lsp-native-attribute-feature-ownership.md) and paired [5999681226](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/8009#issuecomment-5999681226) retain the actual NoNative failure and require fresh unchanged feature-contract/native/protected qualification, signed delivery and successor release proof. Private8010 writer semantics remain separate. The signed7f7 replay uses bottom8059 plus the sole strict editor-oracle child requiring a verified ordered native Stack; [5999927791](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/8009#issuecomment-5999927791) preserves actual NoNative step success and overall red editor failure, requiring combined fresh full gates before highest-ready protected-prefix admission with no individual auto. A genuine signed548 replay resolves the later actual canonical conflict while retaining every incoming clause and exact reviewed helper/native corpus; no historical gate transfers. After actual signed8052 `6f17283b`, the same bottom genuinely follows the moved definition and gates only it and its reexport, preserving complete bodies and broader non-native helpers; [6000519610](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/8009#issuecomment-6000519610) retains obsolete full-run cancellation without current runtime credit and requires one combined exact-source full Stack qualification before protected-prefix delivery. The complete owned clause moves away from the shared final-line hotspot without importing any unmerged tail. The actual94a combined full then fails on the new span getter reexport before tests; [6000677996](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/8009#issuecomment-6000677996) gates only its native external reexport while preserving the ordinary internally used definition, original corpus and strict editor oracle, requiring fresh source/combined full before any prefix admission. After actual signed runner repair #8066 `2902dc98751b408e803ee663aeee494ffab8413b`, the same bottom genuinely incorporates that main while preserving exact helper bodies, original native/corpus and complete incoming clauses; [6002889417](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/8009#issuecomment-6002889417) retains frozen440 full/source infrastructure failures and scoped successful native/NoNative/editor evidence without successor transfer, requiring the sole child literal replay, fresh exact normal/full and ordered native protected-prefix signed delivery before release. [Shared editor reference options](./2026-10-06-shared-editor-reference-options.md) records the post-8116 #7698 successor: canonical editor opens and native diagnostics share owned configured options cloned before existing declaration discovery. Preserve full declarations, strong source/config authority, watcher refresh, original Nuxt controls and all caps; exact-source native mirror/full-payload continuity and original400 first/warm discovery cost remain unexecuted, with no PID/API-project, gain or private-project claim. Paired shared-reference decision: https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/7698#issuecomment-6015282967; current native continuity and original400 cost remain pending. The actual 3f46 helper Clippy failure and separate 337 release assembly failure remain failed; [6015578882](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/7698#issuecomment-6015578882) preserves the original three-diagnostic law and adds only contextual helper assertions plus test-thread full prefilter/generated/decoded-native/error/config/stamp/elapsed custody. No original input, native request, timeout, retry, fallback or expected vector changes; the empty-editor cause and current full qualification remain unresolved, with no full native-wire claim. The separate Element Plus complete hover contract is string, as independently derived from the unchanged authored content payload and slot factory; [6015819899](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/7698#issuecomment-6015819899) corrects only that obsolete any literal/comment while preserving all original inputs and other assertions. Fresh full qualification and the original empty-assembly cause remain pending. Canon transfers already-owned native Vue/script document batches to its existing worker without a second full URI/text copy; [the source record](./2026-10-06-owned-native-document-batches.md) and paired [6019045579](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/7698#issuecomment-6019045579) preserve the exact borrowed API, full native synchronization, original400 inputs, epochs, cancellation, deadlines and current instruction ceilings, with measured benefit still unknown. Historical `718` source/native/full400 contracts pass but its one shipping pair demonstrates no hover speed benefit; [6033188073](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/7698#issuecomment-6033188073) records genuine actual security-main `ef848` incorporation, preserves all incoming decisions and requires fresh exact-head qualification before protected signed delivery, with no historical execution transfer. Historical `428` passes 16,509 source records and the whole native/original400 contracts but fails its new MCP audit; [6034041575](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/7698#issuecomment-6034041575) records actual signed `706a` incorporation with every incoming byte preserved and requires fresh exact-head qualification, without general latency or attribution to the old `337` failure.

- **Levels are type boundaries, not runtime boundaries.**
  - There is no serialization between levels; dumps exist only for `vize dump` and observers.
  - All artifacts share one arena and use dense `u32` ids.
  - Passes are fused into single walks where possible.
  - Anything unobserved costs nothing (generic observers; entity decoding only when `&` is present; incremental keys only in resident mode).
- **Regressions are stopped in the merge queue** with instruction-count measurements per stage ([#6868](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6868)). The wall-clock envelope runs nightly. The [Nuxt bridge benchmark entry repair](./2026-10-05-nuxt-bridge-benchmark-entry.md), paired with #7846, resolves its unchanged three provider exports from the real repository-root module and retains every workload, default, timing implementation and published result. One ten-module/one-pass external-cwd Actions control verifies complete stage output and truthful optional-unimport absence; it provides entry correctness, no end-to-end build or performance gain. Actual merge and publication remain pending.
- **Per-stage budgets ratchet from current measurements.** Today all 102 `wall_p50_ns` entries in `plan/budgets.toml` are unset. The [authored allocation-window decision](./2026-10-02-authored-allocation-window.md) preserves every cold-parse and markup ceiling while executing the process-global measurement gate directly outside libtest's concurrent result-channel bookkeeping; its static inventory report installs the root's locked TOML parser with the existing pinned runtime setup, and its workflow law verifies the named setup, install, collection and upload sequence without relaxing the final aggregate. Its single-case CLI now follows nextest's discovery and exact-selection protocol: listing performs no measurement, selected execution retains the original body, and callback assertions propagate failure. Earlier direct Cargo executions remain valid observations, while nextest runtime credit requires fresh discovery and worker evidence. The [standalone runtime ledger](../plan/custom-harness-runtime-2026-10-02.md) separates all four source-bound nonbench registrations from benchmark targets and records missing nextest discovery, owning dispatcher repairs, and required assigned-worker evidence.
- The [instruction-count gate record](./2026-09-27-instruction-counts.md) defines measured-only ceilings and immutable-base ratchets for #6868. Independent clean Actions builds (run 36307058591, attempts 1 and 2) match all 100 probes in three executions each under the fixed guest method. Required `test-report` aggregates queue measurement and strict ceilings. Separate queue tests and test-inventory collection preserve its check name and satisfy the source-length ratchet. Exact queue verification is pending. The [completed-window hotspot diagnostic](./2026-10-01-instruction-hotspots.md) reports existing Callgrind function costs for actual over-budget workloads before the unchanged strict comparator; it adds no runs, stages or ceiling changes. Actual x86-64 output costs motivate the [bounded indentation repair](./2026-10-01-native-indent-perf.md); initial batching holds all one hundred fixed ceilings, and its lint correction must repeat exact-head Actions without changing any measurement identity or ceiling. [Bounded current-source latency/RSS](./2026-10-05-lsp-current-source-latency.md) defines one manually reviewed Misskey baseline: three strict-source fresh sessions, unchanged original cycles/oracles/caps, 120 explicit extra completions outside cycle timers, complete monotonic RPC and independent sampled process-tree custody. Actual hosted measurements, GUI responsiveness, shared-refresh causality, native/default/history and 10x acceptance remain unfinished; #7835 mapping and #7824 unopened-file regression oracles are the next separate real-RPC followups.
- The [#7044 queue correction](./2026-09-27-instruction-counts.md#helper-alias-scan-queue-correction-7044) restores the original comparator and only skips non-underscore bytes in the ordinary alias scan; [#7083](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/7083) tracks the measured single-pass design. Unchanged ceilings decide acceptance.
- **`SideTable` stays `FxHashMap`** ([#6869](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6869), measured 2026-09-28).
  - Density over the repo's 1,419 `.vue` templates (18,283 L2 ops): `static_facts` holds 0.33 facts per op. Every other table holds under 0.05, and `if_facts`/`model_faults` under 0.001.
  - A lookup costs about 2.9 ns hashed and 1.4 ns in a dense `Vec`. The DOM emitter's static-fact reads would save well under 1% of a compile.
  - No table meets the densification trigger in `side_table.rs` (majority occupancy). Re-measure if a table's occupancy passes one half.

## Toolchain practice

[External report attribution](../../../CONTRIBUTING.md#fix-requests) prioritizes reports
through regression, verified co-authors, actual CI/merge and public release verification. [JSON config comments](2026-10-05-formatter-json-comments.md) records #7865: all CLI JSON names select the existing comment-preserving parser while the public strict API and malformed errors remain; sixteen full-output/CLI custody rows await hosted and protected delivery. The a271 actual-main replay preserves every incoming decision, original author/trailer and owned source byte; original hosted failures/successes remain retained and fresh exact-head qualification is required. The actual 8f protected-prefix replay retains every incoming owner and owned source byte; fresh exact-head source/protected reports remain required.

credit nine reports already shipped in v0.429.1 without rewriting fix/tag history;
the four documented workspace-only experiments remain unpublished. Vize follows language-toolchain practice, not compiler-only practice. It stays lightweight and fast. It avoids the heaviness of rust-analyzer-style designs: fine-grained per-node queries, per-node reference-counted trees and whole-workspace resident state. [Linked workspace editor staging](2026-10-06-symlink-workspace-editor-staging.md) records #7990: preserve the entire six-file original and queued open order, restore only the prior completed private snapshot’s exact link identities for unchanged root-first retirement checks, and let current whole-union owned files supersede stale external package links without authored writes/cleanup or resolution/native-path changes. Require those exact two regular cache entries and compare every other whole entry; all original inputs/diagnostics/waits/production remain unchanged and fresh all-session qualification is pending. ## CI tiers

Tracked in [#6830](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6830) and [#6861](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6861)–[#6867](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/6867). [Nix parser facade preparation](./2026-10-03-nix-parser-facade-dummy.md) preserves both real parser identities during manifest-only dependency builds after #7501. The [devalue security patch](./2026-10-02-devalue-security.md) records #7383's unchanged production audit gate and minimal serializer update. The [Node Forge source repair](./2026-10-02-node-forge-security.md) binds only the actual Node listhen verifier to exact patch/registry/lock and published-source identities, distinguishing exactly four observed generated launchers after a matching Linux census. Real crypto/TLS laws and both audit JSON contracts pass; unknown moderate-or-higher findings or consumers remain fatal, browser distributions remain unpatched, and final installed-head/full acceptance is pending. The [Wasmtime host patch](./2026-10-03-wasmtime-security-patch.md) records three new RustSec findings and actual Cargo 1.98 resolver output for the exact 48.0.4 pin: 33 affected-family records, 491 byte-exact other package blocks and no added/removed edges. Required/full product audits ended red on old 48.0.3; the product stays unqueued until fresh unchanged audit/Contracts/full/Fuzz/all-100 gates accept the generated closure. The [Braces depth backport](./2026-10-03-braces-depth-security.md) records the distinct new GHSA-vfj7-8cjw-p6xm blocker and the narrow open-PR depth-hunk adaptation, preserving registry quote/comma and stringify behavior. Actual-source bounded and ordinary-output laws pass; genuine pnpm output supplies only the Braces hash/snapshot/two edges while every unrelated native/Forge byte is conserved. The exact projected lock passes inherited vp frozen installation with 34 Linux package/consumer/range file hashes, real linked depth laws and unchanged Forge proof. The compound validator accepts the original two-HIGH report with its LOW count visible; unknown actionable findings remain fatal. The first ordinary source security audit passes; strict type-aware lint rejects an implicit unknown-ID sort, repaired only by an explicit comparator preserving raw identity validation. Fresh corrected-head ordinary and protected-queue acceptance remain pending. [Wrapped npm staged diagnostics](./2026-10-06-npm-staged-diagnostic-wrapping.md) records #8054: retain the full authenticated five-line wrapped409 stderr and old helper fixture, fold only diagnostic ASCII whitespace and border for the exact staged-version phrase, copy the StringView at the recognition boundary after source Actions error4014, preserve all publisher arguments/output/registry polling bounds and generic errors, qualify eight complete Moon helper controls on Actions, and keep the next-batch PR Draft/unqueued during first0.433 publication; actual merge and next release remain pending.

| Tier           | Runs              | Target                   | Content                                                                                                                                                                            |
| -------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| T0 PR          | every push        | p50 ≤ 3 min, p90 ≤ 6 min | fmt, title-policy, clippy and tests for affected crates (nextest archive + shards), input-selected tooling tests                                                                   |
| T1 merge queue | once before merge | —                        | full workspace and tooling tests, differential corpus and acceptance gates, instruction-count performance gates, playground ([#6865 execution](./2026-09-27-merge-queue-gates.md)) |
| T2 nightly     | schedule          | —                        | E2E, real-project matrix, fuzz, miri, benchmarks, editor conformance, resource budgets                                                                                             |
| T3 release     | release           | —                        | everything, semver checks, release preflight                                                                                                                                       |

- zizmor runs only for external contributors, releases and PRs that touch `.github/**`. Content Mapper frame stability for #3984 is recorded in [Bound Content Mapper frame headers](./2026-10-08-content-mapper-frame-bounds.md). # Bound Content Mapper frame headers Owning issue: [#3984](https://github.com/ubugeeei-prod/vize/issues/3984). The standard `vize content-mapper` stdio reader previously bounded only the message body. `read_until` could allocate an unlimited header line, many short headers could accumulate without a total bound, and a repeated Content-Length silently replaced the first length. These are source-owned framing stability gaps, independent of the native TypeScript semantic and declaration gates. The reader now limits each frame's complete headers to 8 KiB, including every line ending and the terminating blank line. A limited read enforces the remaining allowance before an arbitrarily large line can be accumulated. A complete header block at exactly 8 KiB is accepted. An exhausted allowance without a complete terminator is rejected without consuming body or subsequent-frame bytes. A second case-insensitive Content-Length is rejected before its value is parsed, whether it repeats, contradicts or invalidates the first length. The existing 64 MiB body bound, CRLF/LF handling, unknown-header handling and JSON-RPC project and transform semantics remain unchanged. Fragmented reads retain Interrupted retry behavior through the standard library's bounded reader. The regression corpus retains fourteen whole raw stdio inputs with independent input and complete response hashes. Public CLI tests compare exact stdout, stderr and exit status for three write fragmentation sizes. Controls cover clean EOF, concatenated CRLF/LF frames, the exact header boundary, one-byte and many-line overflow, equal and conflicting duplicate lengths, missing/invalid lengths, truncated headers/body, the unchanged body ceiling, and a valid response before a later fatal frame. Reader laws additionally retain UTF-8 body bytes, arbitrary internal buffer fragmentation, interrupted reads and precise consumption bounds. A focused standalone harness extracted from the actual old reader demonstrates two failing framing laws; the changed reader passes those laws and healthy controls. This is local reader evidence only. The source-authenticated public CLI and full existing protocol tests require fresh exact-head Actions, followed by root-owned protected qualification and actual merge. No historical or local binary substitutes for that evidence; no instruction ceiling is raised. The complete tsconfig graph, declarations, native mapper semantics, LSP parity, installed-consumer replay and the typechecker's 10x speed target remain unfinished where their own required witnesses are absent. This bounded change does not close #3984 or qualify n8n JSONC/project transport. The separate original-source JSONC authority proposal remains read-only with its bridge owner; upstream n8n and TypeScript stay read-only.
